# dual_copy_allowlist.txt -- the burn-down list for `scripts/lint_rva_bodies.py`
#
# Every line here is an RVA that currently has MORE THAN ONE body (or more than
# one active install). The guard WARNs on these and FAILS on anything new. The
# list is meant to shrink to zero: ROADMAP phase D4 consolidates each pair into
# ONE shared TU judged against `original/MASHED.exe`.
#
# Seeded 2026-09-29 from `py -3.12 scripts/lint_rva_bodies.py --seed` at commit
# bd367bc1, on the findings of `re/analysis/DUAL_COPY_AUDIT_2026-09-29.md`.
# Rationale and burn-down plan: `re/analysis/DUAL_COPY_FIX_2026-09-29.md`.
#
# Format: <CHECK>:<rva-8hex>:<comma-separated sorted TU paths>   # citation
#   DUP-IN-TARGET  two bodies compiled into the SAME target
#   CROSS-TARGET   a body in an exe-only TU and a body in an asi-only TU
#   DUP-INSTALL    two ACTIVE RH_ScopedInstall for one RVA in .asi TUs (U-9065 class)
#
# ORIGTOP-ONLY class (HB-LINT-ORIGTOP-CLASS, L-111 (a), 2026-11-09). A line prefixed `ORIGTOP-ONLY ` is NOT part of
# the D4 burn-down: it is a byte-matched body that runs only under MASHED_ORIG_TOP (the matched WinMain 0x00492370
# route), sitting beside the hand body the default path still runs. The lint accepts such a line only when exactly
# one body of the pair is in an EXE-ONLY TU carrying the marker line `// RVA-LINT: ORIGTOP-ONLY` and the other is
# not; a tag on any other finding, or a class pair on an untagged line, fails the build. The lint prints the D4
# burn-down count and the ORIGTOP-ONLY count separately.
#   Format: ORIGTOP-ONLY <CHECK>:<rva-8hex>:<comma-separated sorted TU paths>   # citation
# THE WHOLE CLASS IS DELETED AT THE ORIG_TOP FLIP: when the matched route becomes the default, the hand bodies go,
# every ORIGTOP-ONLY line goes with them, and the marker lines are removed from the TUs.
#
# The `audit=` tag is the verdict that row carried in the 2026-09-29 audit CSV.
# `audit=UNREVIEWED` and rows with no tag are STRUCTURAL CANDIDATES that no one
# has compared body-to-body yet -- they are allowlisted so the guard can go live,
# NOT because they are known-safe.
#
# ADDING A LINE IS A DECISION. Do not silence a new finding here to get a build
# through: the whole point is that the five 2026-09-29 defects would each have
# produced exactly such a line.

# --- ADDED 2026-09-30 (U-9156): exposed by the SECOND ANCHORING PASS in
# scripts/rva_body_scan.py, which binds a body whose `// <rva> <Name>` header
# comment is NOT directly above the definition. Every line below is a body the
# guard could not see before, i.e. the 0x0046b1c0 blind spot repeating. They are
# audit=UNREVIEWED-U9156: STRUCTURAL CANDIDATES, nobody has compared them
# body-to-body. Allowlisted so the guard stays live; burn-down is ROADMAP D4.

# --- ADDED 2026-10-16 (fwd494fd0, ROADMAP D4 item 2, the AI copies). The AI-area keys above went from 11 to the
# three below: harness drivers, call-throughs and prose the scanner had bound as bodies were re-anchored, two dead
# bodies deleted (AiVehicleVelocity3, AiPerFrameTick), CarSlotStateSet consolidated into Ai/CarSlotState.cpp.
# What is left is REAL and none of it is a mechanical merge: the exe body is a different, shorter body. Detail:
# verify/fwd494fd0_20261016/RESULT_fwd494fd0.md section C, and each row's DEMOTED note in hooks.csv.
CROSS-TARGET:004177b0:Ai/AiPreTick.cpp,Race/RuleEngine.cpp   # DELIBERATE until D3 criterion (b). exe-only Race/RuleEngine.cpp:UpdateFinishOrder is the finish-order FRAGMENT of FUN_004177b0 (its own comment: `finish-order fragment`); asi-only Ai/AiPreTick.cpp:AiPreTick is the whole function (verbatim, A/B driver AbAiPreTick). The other exe copy, Ai/AiStandalone.cpp:AiPreTickRubberBand, is invisible to the scanner (no RVA comment directly above it). It is now a structural port of all five phases, so the 2026-09-29 demotion note (which listed the speed scaling, powerup doubling and 0x0089a368 machine as missing) is stale on that point; what remains open is its harvested kDiffProb tables (BODYDIFF rows 30/31, index taken modulo 56) and the sources of its two reads. Both addresses have exe writers: 0x008a9648 is written at PathProgStep.cpp:248/289 under MASHED_PATHPROG and at :240/335 under MASHED_PP_PUBLISH=0x1 (both OFF, so by default its readers see the BSS value), and 0x008a96ec has the default-ON bridge producer (PathProgStep.cpp:337). Body diff (.asi 157 lines, exe 142): verify/d4dc77b0diff_20261030. Value sources: verify/d4dc77b0valsrc_20261030. This is the next AI pair to take through the AiBankSwitch recipe. Row C2.
